PROMPT 1 (15 SEC) — ONE BITE GLAZED DONUT

EXACT 15 SECOND VIDEO. Seedance 2.5. Vertical 9:16. Audio on. One continuous video. Duration is 15 seconds, not 30.

REFERENCE IMAGE URLS:
Big Earl FULL BODY = https://raw.githubusercontent.com/relnoorain2-droid/Big-Earl-Eats-America/main/Redheaded%20Cowboy%20in%20Studio.png
Big Earl FACE = https://raw.githubusercontent.com/relnoorain2-droid/Big-Earl-Eats-America/main/Rugged%20Ginger-Bearded%20Cowboy%20Portrait.png

CHARACTER LOCK: Big Earl must look exactly like the reference images for the full 15 seconds. American man about 40, about 6'5" tall, heavy-set with a big round belly, fair sunburnt skin with freckles, long thick ginger-red beard reaching his chest, short ginger hair, small light-blue eyes, calm deadpan face. Same weathered brown leather cowboy hat, red-and-black buffalo-check flannel shirt with rolled sleeves, light-blue faded jeans, brown belt with a big oval silver buckle, worn brown cowboy boots. Never change his face, beard, hat, outfit, body size or age. Only one Big Earl in the video.

PLACE: a small donut shop counter early morning, a glass case of fresh donuts, warm lights.

CAMERA: Raw real iPhone video. It starts with Big Earl filming himself selfie-style, holding the phone at arm's length in his big hand, then he hands the phone to his friend (never seen). Natural shake, real autofocus, natural light, true-to-life colors. Looks like a real vlog posted on social media, not a movie.

0-2s — HOOK
Big Earl holds the phone up selfie-style, his face filling the frame, the place behind him.
DIALOGUE 0-2s — BIG EARL: "One glazed donut. One bite. Let's see."

2-10s — THE MOMENT
Earl hands the phone to his friend, who turns it to a sleepy teen cashier in a paper hat.
DIALOGUE 3-5s — CASHIER: "That'll be one fifty."
Earl rubs his big hands together slowly. Earl eats the whole glazed donut in one bite, glaze on his beard, and chews slowly.

10-15s — VERDICT
Earl wipes his beard with the back of his hand and looks into the phone. Completely deadpan.
DIALOGUE 11-14s — BIG EARL: "Ten out of ten. Give me eleven more."
The video ends as Earl winks once at the camera.

SOUND: donut case door, register beep, morning traffic. Big Earl's voice: deep, slow, calm American male voice with a light Texas drawl. All lines spoken clearly with lip-sync, in this order. Dialogue louder than background sound. No music.
NEGATIVE: no cinematic look, no movie lighting, no 3D, no cartoon, no slow motion, no music, no subtitles, no text overlays, no watermark, no logo, no face or beard changes, no outfit changes, no extra fingers, no floating food.
